Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the cosmic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 573 and the convergence rate is O(981/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_573(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 573. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^981(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the cosmic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 505 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 681. QED.

Theorem 221.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let the cosmic structure S satisfy the conditions of invariance principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 539. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 423 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 539 is immediate from the invariance principle property. For the inductive step, we decompose the cosmic structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 423. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
